    def _resolver_blocked(self, text: str) -> dict:
        """Drug ids inside the pieces the intervention resolver refuses as a distinct
        product or a tracer, with the refused piece."""
        comps = []
        lone = set()
        pieces = {p.strip() for p in re.split(r"[,;\uff0c\uff1b\u3001/|]", text) if p.strip()}
        for piece in pieces:                         # each distinct list piece once
            words = [w for w in re.split(r"[^\w\-]+", piece) if w]
            if not self._drug_spans(words, set(), [0] * len(words)):
                continue                             # no drug in it: nothing to refuse
            got = self._resolve_cache.get(piece)
            if got is None:
                try:
                    got = N.resolve_drug_string(piece, self.dic, self.index_keys)["components"]
                except Exception:                    # never let a guard crash a lookup
                    got = []
                if len(self._resolve_cache) >= 4096:
                    self._resolve_cache.clear()
                self._resolve_cache[piece] = got
            if len(words) == 1 and T.canonical_key(piece) in self.dic["index"]:
                lone.add(piece)                      # one stored name on its own ("566C")
            comps += got
        out: dict = {}
        accepted = {c.get("drug_id") for c in comps
                    if c.get("drug_id") and not str(c.get("tier", "")).startswith("BLOCKED")}
        for c in comps:
            piece = c.get("component") or ""
            tracer = "radiotracer_guard" in (c.get("rules") or []) or T.is_radiotracer(piece)
            if not (c.get("tier") == "BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT"
                    or (str(c.get("tier", "")).startswith("BLOCKED") and tracer)):
                continue
            if piece in lone:
                continue
            words = [w for w in re.split(r"[\s/|,;]+", piece) if w]
            words = [w.strip(" ?.!:()[]") or w for w in words]
            for a, b in self._drug_spans(words, set(), [0] * len(words)):
                key = T.canonical_key(" ".join(words[a:b]))
                for h in self.dic["index"].get(key, []):
                    # the same molecule named separately and accepted ("biosimilar
                    # anastrozole, Arimidex") is left to the list-item rule below
                    if h["drug_id"] not in accepted:
                        out[h["drug_id"]] = piece
        return out

    def _keys_of(self, did: str) -> set:
        """Every lookup key that names this drug."""
        if not hasattr(self, "_drug_keys"):
            self._drug_keys: dict = {}
            for k, hits in self.dic["index"].items():
                for h in hits:
                    self._drug_keys.setdefault(h["drug_id"], set()).add(k)
        return self._drug_keys.get(did, set())

    def _marked(self, key: str, did: str, tokens, seg, a: int, b: int):
        """The text that makes this drug name a distinct product or a tracer, if any.

        Checked on the same letters-and-digits form the name was looked up by: a known
        biosimilar suffix at the end of the name ("bevacizumab awwb", "bevacizumab_awwb")
        or as the next word, or an isotope label just before or after it ("[89.Zr]
        trastuzumab", "89:Zr trastuzumab")."""
        for sfx in T.BIOSIMILAR_FDA_SUFFIXES:
            stem = key[:-len(sfx)]
            if key.endswith(sfx) and stem and any(
                    h["drug_id"] == did for h in self.dic["index"].get(stem, [])):
                return " ".join(tokens[a:b])
        same = [k for k in range(len(tokens)) if seg[k] == seg[a]]
        before = [k for k in same if k < a]
        after = [k for k in same if k >= b]
        # words counted after normalisation, so punctuation never uses up the budget
        prev = _alnum_words(" ".join(tokens[k] for k in before))[-2:]
        nxt = _alnum_words(" ".join(tokens[k] for k in after))[:2]
        own = _alnum_words(" ".join(tokens[a:b]))
        lone = not before and not after      # the name is the whole list item
        # an isotope label anywhere in the name ("A33 124i"), or just before or after it
        # ("[89.Zr] trastuzumab", "trastuzumab 89 - Zr"); an isotope-shaped name counts
        # as a name only when it stands alone ("566C", "18f"), never as the substance
        # of a longer phrase ("18F fluorothymidine")
        inside = [own[i:j] for i in range(len(own)) for j in (i + 1, i + 2) if j <= len(own)]
        # and every run of the name's words glued as the key glues them, so a symbol
        # split by punctuation inside a stored alias is still an isotope ("[89Z.r]-DFO-")
        for i in range(len(own)):
            for j in range(i + 1, len(own) + 1):
                glued = "".join(own[i:j])
                if len(glued) > 6:
                    break
                if j - i > 1:
                    inside.append([glued])
        if lone and T.is_isotope_label("".join(own)):
            inside = []                      # the whole item is one isotope-shaped name
        name = " ".join(tokens[a:b])
        # the neighbours glued back together the way the lookup key glues them, so a
        # suffix or an isotope spelt in pieces is still seen ("a - w w b", "[89Z.r]")
        nxt_all = _alnum_words(" ".join(tokens[k] for k in after))[:8]
        prev_all = _alnum_words(" ".join(tokens[k] for k in before))[-8:]
        for n in range(1, len(nxt_all) + 1):
            glued = "".join(nxt_all[:n]).lower()
            if glued in T.BIOSIMILAR_FDA_SUFFIXES or T.is_isotope_label(glued):
                return f"{name} ({' '.join(nxt_all[:n])})"
            if len(glued) > 6:
                break
        for n in range(1, len(prev_all) + 1):
            glued = "".join(prev_all[-n:]).lower()
            if T.is_isotope_label(glued):
                return f"({' '.join(prev_all[-n:])}) {name}"
            if len(glued) > 6:
                break
        # a label fused to a neighbouring word, such as a chelator ("DFO89Zr", "89ZrDFO")
        for w in nxt_all[:2] + prev_all[-2:]:
            if _isotope_edge(w):
                return f"{name} ({w})"
        for ws in inside + [prev[-1:], prev, nxt[:1], nxt]:
            if ws and T.is_isotope_label(" ".join(ws)):
                return name if ws in inside else f"{name} ({' '.join(ws)})"
        # an isotope glued to the name itself ("89zr-trastuzumab" read through "[89Z.r]")
        # anything may sit between them (a chelator: "89ZrDFO-daratumumab"), so the rest
        # needs only to end, or start, with a name of the same drug
        # A medical isotope anywhere in the list item once its letters and digits are run
        # together, however it is split or cased ("89-ZrDFO trastuzumab", "3hvinblastine",
        # "trastuzumab Ga68"), refuses an unlabelled drug. A drug whose own name carries a
        # label (LUTETIUM LU 177 DOTATATE, FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE F 18) is the labelled product.
        if (_item_has_isotope(tuple(tokens[k] for k in same))
                and not _labelled(self.dic["drugs"][did]["name"])):
            return name
        # A one-letter element is left to the case-marked word check above: lower-case
        # chemistry ("13-cis retinoic acid") is not carbon-13.
        # The drug's name may sit anywhere in the rest ("89Zrpanitumumab-PET-CT"). A
        # one-letter element counts where it is written upper-case in the name
        # ("3HVINBLASTINE", "A33124I"); lower-case chemistry ("13-cis") does not.
        names = [k for k in self._keys_of(did) if len(k) >= 5]
        orig = "".join(re.findall(r"[A-Za-z0-9]+", _plain(" ".join(tokens[a:b]))))
        if len(orig) != len(key):
            orig = key.upper()                   # cannot align: treat as marked
        for i in range(2, min(6, len(key))):
            for lab, tail, at in ((key[:i], key[i:], i - 1), (key[-i:], key[:-i], len(key) - 1)):
                if not tail or not T.is_isotope_label(lab):
                    continue
                elem = re.sub(r"^\d+m?", "", lab)
                if len(elem) == 1 and not orig[at].isupper():
                    continue
                if any(k in tail for k in names):
                    return name
                # a label closing a stored name of this drug ("A33124I"): the name itself

# Round 20 adversarial name review

Current HEAD: `0932ac3`. **One new P1 common-cause blocker: absent radioactive elements allow labelled trastuzumab to become the originator at every stage.** This is an inherited hole, not a regression introduced by HEAD. Exact M19-B1/B2 repairs pass. No tracked files were edited; scripts and evidence are confined to this directory. The database connection for probes is `mode=ro`.

## M20-B1: 161Tb / terbium-161 labelled product selects unlabelled trastuzumab

Exact strongest input: **`trastuzumab labelled with terbium-161`**. Current direct map returns **TRASTUZUMAB / CHEMBL1201585 / 13 trials**, with no refusal. `Which trials evaluate trastuzumab labelled with terbium-161?` and `Who sponsors trials of trastuzumab labelled with terbium-161?` each return **12 originator evidence rows**. The paired `trastuzumab labelled with lutetium-177` refuses, with no map centre and no QA evidence.

This is a real research product, not an invented isotope/drug pairing. The primary study of Cold et al. reports DOTA-functionalized trastuzumab radiolabelled with 161Tb and compares it with native trastuzumab. It was published **2026-04-01**, before this review date. [PubMed primary abstract](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41679917/). The equivalence of the explicit plain-English input to that labelled product is the reviewer's inference; no clinical recommendation or graph evidence is taken from the paper.

| Stage | `trastuzumab labelled with terbium-161` | `trastuzumab labelled with lutetium-177` |
|---|---|---|
| Intervention resolver | CONTAINED_ALIAS + UNRESOLVED; no BLOCKED | CONTAINED_ALIAS + UNRESOLVED; no BLOCKED |
| `_resolver_blocked` | `{}` | `{}` |
| `_qualified` | false | false |
| `_item_has_isotope` | false | true |
| Actual `_marked` call | `None`, all four tokens in segment 0 | refusal |
| `_whole_name` | `None` | `None` |
| Public linker | positive Drug, no Refused | Refused only |
| Direct map | CHEMBL1201585, 13 trials | refused, no centre |
| Trial / sponsor QA | 12 / 12 evidence rows | 0 / 0 |

**Cause:** the common isotope vocabulary omits `tb` in `src/textrules.py:290-293`, and `src/query.py:124-128` omits its masses. The full-name table `src/query.py:156-162` omits `terbium`. Consequently neither the symbol rules nor the new full-name loop (`src/query.py:182-188`) can recognize it. `labelled` alone is not one of the radioactive words (`src/query.py:164-180`). The item-wide guard in `_marked` therefore returns no refusal (`src/query.py:424-426`), leaving a positive Drug for `Engine.explore` (`src/app.py:88-95`). This is broader than a punctuation spelling: any newly used element absent from all three tables misses every layer.

Corroborations in [full stage evidence](new_results.json), [compact results](new_summary.json): `161Tb trastuzumab`, `Tb-161 trastuzumab`, `[161Tb] trastuzumab`, `terbium-161 trastuzumab`, `161-terbium trastuzumab`, `trastuzumab labelled with 161-terbium`, `161Tb-labelled trastuzumab`, `trastuzumab labelled with Tb-161`, `trastuzumab labelled with 161Tb`, and `161Tb Herceptin` all draw the same originator and return 12 / 12 QA rows. `[161Tb] trastuzumab` is especially notable: intervention resolution reports **EXACT**, after losing the unrecognised bracketed isotope. `161Tb radiolabelled trastuzumab` correctly refuses via the explicit label word. The completely fused `161Tb-trastuzumab` and `[161Tb]Tb-trastuzumab` are **not_found** and are not claimed as leaks.

The same omission reproduces for `227Th trastuzumab`, `trastuzumab labelled with thorium-227`, `213Bi trastuzumab`, and `trastuzumab labelled with bismuth-213`: all 13 map trials / 12 trial evidence / 12 sponsor evidence, no refusal. These elements are also absent. The 227Th product was independently compared with cold trastuzumab in [Abbas et al., 2011](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/22214432/); a [primary 213Bi-Herceptin experiment](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/15670895/) establishes the other labelled construct. [Supplement stage evidence](supplement_results.json). These are one table-coverage blocker, not separately counted defects. Scandium/holmium probes are recorded as software observations but are not needed to establish the finding.

All these inputs also leak with `HEAD^:src/query.py` substituted against current remaining modules/data. This is a counterfactual module comparison, not a complete historical deployment. Root independently owns real CLI HTTP corroboration.

## Label-word/metastable focus and regressions

- **M19-B1 FIXED:** `radio-labelled trastuzumab` and `radio-labeled trastuzumab` now refuse; prior query module draws the originator. The joined `radiolabelled trastuzumab` control refuses in both.
- **M19-B2 FIXED:** `trastuzumab labelled with 99m-technetium`, `99m technetium trastuzumab` now refuse; `trastuzumab labelled with technetium-99m` and `technetium 99m trastuzumab` also refuse.
- **Inherited supplements NOT FIXED:** `trastuzumab radioactively labelled`, `radioconjugated trastuzumab`, `trastuzumab labelled with iodine124`, `99mtechnetium trastuzumab`, and `trastuzumab labelled with technetium99m` still draw TRASTUZUMAB. These were already recorded in m19; they are not presented as newly discovered regressions.
- **Additional spelling supplement:** `radio-immuno-conjugate trastuzumab` and `radio-immuno-conjugated trastuzumab` draw TRASTUZUMAB; `radio-immunoconjugate trastuzumab` and `radioimmunoconjugate trastuzumab` refuse. The glued-neighbour rule only forms pairs, leaving the three-part spelling unmatched (`src/query.py:179-180`). This extends the known lexical-recognition weakness; it is not separately counted as a new blocker.
- **M20-S1 new over-refusal spelling / should-fix:** `non-radio-labelled trastuzumab` now refuses while the previous query module draws TRASTUZUMAB. `unlabelled trastuzumab` remains available. Pair joining ignores the `non-` negation. This is a newly affected spelling of M19-S1, not an originator leak or approval blocker.
- Ordinary controls `trastuzumab`, `Herceptin`, `cisplatin and radiation`, `radiotherapy plus cisplatin`, `osimertinib 80 mg`, `paclitaxel 2 cycles`, and `lead compound osimertinib` retain their expected molecules. `fluorine-18 fluorodeoxyglucose` retains FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE F 18.
- `FDG labelled with carbon-14` continues to select the F18 preferred product. This is the inherited compatibility concern; it is not promoted to an independently evidenced product-identity blocker here.

import json, os, subprocess, sys
from pathlib import Path
OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT=OUT.parents[2]
rows=[]
cases=[('plain',n) for n in [50,100,200,250,500,1000,2000]]+ [('split',n) for n in [250,1000,2000]]+[('single',500),('drug',244),('late_qualifier',235)]

        env={**os.environ,'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE':'1','PYTHONUTF8':'1'},capture_output=True,timeout=15)
    assert p.returncode==0,p.stderr
    row=json.loads(p.stdout); rows.append(row)
    (OUT/'perf_current.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
    print(json.dumps(row),flush=True)

# Independent challenge of M20-B1

**Conclusion: retain one P1 blocker. No contradictory HTTP or stage evidence found.** This check re-read existing captures only; it made no new service calls. [Executable cross-check](independent_check.py), [assertions/results](independent_check.json).

**Counts and identity are verified, not inferred from status 200.** Root's original CLI captures contain 92 main + 28 supplemental requests, all 200. For each of `trastuzumab labelled with terbium-161`, `161Tb trastuzumab`, `Tb-161 trastuzumab`, and `[161Tb] trastuzumab`, the complete HTTP map response is identical to bare `trastuzumab`: CHEMBL1201585, 13 trials, no refusal. Each trial and sponsor answer has 12 evidence rows; both complete evidence arrays are identical to those returned for bare trastuzumab. Centre identity and QA arrays also equal the independent direct stage probe. This is selection of the stored parent molecule, not merely a displayed alias or an unrelated 12-row coincidence.

**Literal spelling is not overstated.** `[161Tb] trastuzumab` leaks: `/resolve` reports EXACT with `extract_parenthetical`, having removed the bracketed isotope. The separate query-stage trace still receives `161Tb` and `trastuzumab` in one segment, but `_marked` returns `None`; `_qualified`, `_item_has_isotope`, and `_resolver_blocked` miss, and `_whole_name` adds no replacement. Conversely, the exact fused spelling `[161Tb]Tb-Trastuzumab` is `not_found` and yields no QA evidence. The report must not call that fused spelling a map leak. `[177Lu] trastuzumab` and `trastuzumab labelled with lutetium-177` both refuse and produce zero QA evidence.

**The product interpretation is supported.** A primary experiment describes trastuzumab conjugated to DOTA then radiolabelled with 161Tb, with native trastuzumab as a separate comparator; it predates this audit. [Cold et al., J Nucl Med, 2026-04-01](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/41679917/). The plain-English `labelled with terbium-161` input unambiguously specifies the modified product; it is stronger evidence than merely placing an unknown code next to a drug. No claim depends on the source's efficacy conclusions.

**Contract caveat is explicit.** Read in isolation, “a name the question linker refuses” is conditional and would not prohibit a bug shared by every guard. This round expressly requests attacks where **every stage misses a refusal**, and the approval context excludes distinct labelled products being drawn as originators. Prior repair notes enforce that same interpretation. Therefore the missing-symbol/name/mass coverage is within the requested review scope; describing it only as an internal `explore` conditional violation would be imprecise.

**No accepted exception applies.** This is an under-refusal for an unlabelled trastuzumab parent, not any recorded over-refusal, not a same-item collateral refusal, not the explicitly labelled FDG/Lu-177 preferred-name exception, and not the bevacizumab biosimilar-synonym limitation. It also reproduces with the prior query module, so label it an inherited hole newly demonstrated in M20—not a new HEAD regression.

+"""Assemble the independent round-20 review from fresh result files."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import json, posixpath, re
+OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+def read(p): return (OUT/p).read_text(encoding='utf8')
+def data(p): return json.loads(read(p))
+def links(text,prefix):
+    def replace(m):
+        target=m.group(1)
+        if re.match(r'https?://|#',target): return m.group(0)
+        return ']('+posixpath.normpath(prefix+'/'+target)+')'
+    return re.sub(r'\]\(([^)]+)\)',replace,text)
+

+from pathlib import Path
+import datetime, hashlib, json, subprocess
+OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT=OUT.parents[1]
+def git(*args): return subprocess.check_output(['git',*args],cwd=ROOT).decode('utf8')
+def digest(p): return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+snapshot=OUT/'integrity_before.json'
+assert not snapshot.exists()
+hashes={p:digest(ROOT/p) for p in git('ls-files','-z').split('\0') if p}
+snapshot.write_text(json.dumps(dict(time=datetime.datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(),head=git('rev-parse','HEAD').strip(),status=git('status','--short'),tracked_sha256=hashes,db_sha256=digest(ROOT/'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite')),indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+manifest=[]
+for family in ['root_checks','cost']:
+    dest=OUT/family
+    dest.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    (dest/'tmp').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    for source in (OUT.parent/'m19'/family).glob('*.py'):
+        target=dest/source.name
+        assert not target.exists(),str(target)
+        target.write_bytes(source.read_bytes())
+        manifest.append(dict(source=source.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),copy=target.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),source_sha256=digest(source),copy_sha256=digest(target),changes=[],note='Output uses __file__; original inputs retained.'))
+(OUT/'reuse_manifest_root.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+print(json.dumps(dict(copies=len(manifest),tracked_files=len(hashes),head=git('rev-parse','HEAD').strip())))

+from pathlib import Path
+import json, os, subprocess, sys, time
+OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT=OUT.parents[2]
+env=dict(os.environ,PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1',PYTHONUTF8='1',PYTHONIOENCODING='utf-8',TMP=str(OUT/'tmp'),TEMP=str(OUT/'tmp'),TMPDIR=str(OUT/'tmp'))
+jobs=[['run_replays.py','http'],['run_remaining.py'],['new_leaks_http.py'],['run_m18_late.py'],['independent_m18_http.py'],['independent_m19_http.py']]
+rows=[]
+for args in jobs:
+    start=time.perf_counter()
+    p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-B',str(OUT/args[0]),*args[1:]],cwd=ROOT,env=env,capture_output=True,timeout=900)
+    (OUT/(args[0]+'.m20.stdout.txt')).write_bytes(p.stdout+p.stderr)
+    row=dict(args=args,exit_code=p.returncode,seconds=time.perf_counter()-start)
+    rows.append(row)
+    (OUT/'execution20.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+    print(json.dumps(row),flush=True)
+    if p.returncode: raise SystemExit(p.returncode)

Counterfactuals replace only historical query methods/modules against current app/data, not whole historical deployments.

### M16-S1: the item's isotope state is now computed once

**FIXED for the exact reported extra work.** At 125 / 1,000 occurrences, instrumentation records **one whole-item scan** and **250 / 2,000 within-word helper calls**, respectively. M16 originally recorded 125 / 1,000 whole-item scans and 15,625 / 1,000,000 within-word calls. M17 recorded one scan with 125 / 1,000 within-word calls. The preceding M18 repair checks both the canonical word and its punctuation piece, which are identical for `isa`; this doubles those within-word calls but does not restore per-match whole-item rescanning. `@functools.lru_cache(maxsize=4096)` caches the pure tuple-keyed computation (`src/query.py:168-199`), called from `_marked` at `src/query.py:424-426`. The same single ISATUXIMAB entity survives. [Fresh instrumentation](cost/repeated_match_instrumentation.json), [reused script](cost/repeated_match_followup.py).

For the legal 3,999-character **answer** field, the fresh HTTP check takes **2.34808 s**, queues EGFR for **2.26631 s**, and takes **2.94029 s** warm. The ordinary map after completion takes 0.04229 s; all return 200. This verifies the repair without claiming all repeated-match work was removed. `_marked` still constructs/scans token lists before checking the cache and runs before entity deduplication (`src/query.py:369-397,582-592`). [HTTP](cost/isolated_repeated_match_cost_http.json), [closed owned server / unchanged DB](cost/repeated_match_cost_lifecycle.json).

`engine.lock` serializes API operations (`src/app.py:247-263`). These 3,999/4,000-character workloads use direct linking or the **answer field**; explore correctly rejects more than 500 characters. Limits precede NFKC (`src/app.py:239-243,260`; `src/query.py:117-118,282-284`). Residual costs are should-fix observations, not the reason for the verdict. **No model calls or model cost were incurred.**
